===== ProfitLockPolicy =====
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   4: 
   5: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   6: import java.math.RoundingMode;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * Pure Dynamic Profit Lock policy shared by live production and Proven/Regression.
  10:  * Keep all percentage/progression math here so replay can never drift from production.
  11:  */
  12: @Component
  13: public class ProfitLockPolicy {
  14:     private static final BigDecimal HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
  15:     private static final int SCALE = 12;
  16: 
  17:     public State evaluate(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal target, BigDecimal current,
  18:                           BigDecimal previousHighest, boolean previouslyActive, BigDecimal previousLock,
  19:                           boolean enabled, BigDecimal activationPercent, BigDecimal initialLockPercent,
  20:                           BigDecimal trailStepPercent) {
  21:         if (entry == null || target == null || current == null || entry.signum() <= 0 || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) {
  22:             return new State(previousHighest, previouslyActive, previousLock, BigDecimal.ZERO, false);
  23:         }
  24: 
  25:         BigDecimal highest = previousHighest;
  26:         if (highest == null || highest.compareTo(entry) < 0) highest = entry;
  27:         if (current.compareTo(highest) > 0) highest = current;
  28:         if (!enabled) return new State(highest, false, null, BigDecimal.ZERO, false);
  29: 
  30:         BigDecimal distance = target.subtract(entry);
  31:         BigDecimal progress = highest.subtract(entry).multiply(HUNDRED)
  32:                 .divide(distance, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).max(BigDecimal.ZERO);
  33:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(activationPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
  34:         BigDecimal initial = nvl(initialLockPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(40));
  35:         BigDecimal step = nvl(trailStepPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(10));
  36: 
  37:         boolean active = previouslyActive;
  38:         BigDecimal lock = previousLock;
  39:         if (progress.compareTo(activation) >= 0) {
  40:             active = true;
  41:             BigDecimal completed = progress.subtract(activation).max(BigDecimal.ZERO)
  42:                     .divide(step, 0, RoundingMode.DOWN);
  43:             BigDecimal lockedProgress = initial.add(completed.multiply(step));
  44:             BigDecimal maximum = progress.subtract(step).max(initial);
  45:             lockedProgress = lockedProgress.min(maximum).max(initial);
  46:             BigDecimal candidate = entry.add(distance.multiply(lockedProgress)
  47:                     .divide(HUNDRED, SCALE, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
  48:             candidate = candidate.max(entry.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005)));
  49:             if (lock == null || candidate.compareTo(lock) > 0) lock = candidate;
  50:         }
  51:         boolean triggered = active && lock != null && current.compareTo(lock) <= 0;
  52:         return new State(highest, active, lock, progress, triggered);
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     private static BigDecimal nvl(BigDecimal value, BigDecimal fallback) {
  56:         return value == null ? fallback : value;
  57:     }
  58: 
  59:     public record State(BigDecimal highestPrice, boolean active, BigDecimal lockPrice,
  60:                         BigDecimal progressPercent, boolean triggered) {}
  61: }

  25: /**
  26:  * Mechanical protection for an already-open position using live Binance prices.
  27:  * TP / SL / Profit Lock must not wait for the next analysis signal.
  28:  */
  29: @Service
  30: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  31: @Slf4j
  32: public class LivePositionProtectionService {
  33:     // FIX-125 mandatory in Spring; null only in existing constructor-only policy tests.

  50:     // FIX-153: direct observations use atomic effects+outcome; shared callers retain their ledger API.
  51:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired private com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService priceEvents;
  52:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  53:     @Transactional(isolation=org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Isolation.READ_COMMITTED)
  54:     public void onPrice(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observedAt,long eventId){
  55:         price=com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(price);
  56:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
  57:         // Acquire the existing managed-position lock BEFORE freshness, including waits on it.
  58:         try {managedPositionRepository.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol,"OPEN");}
  59:         catch(RuntimeException failure){
  60:             // FIX-153: preserve only the established pre-business position-deadlock retry permission.
  61:             if(com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure))
  62:                 throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
  63:             throw failure;
  64:         }
  65:         Instant localNow=Instant.now();
  66:         String rejection=priceEvents.invalidReason(price,observedAt,exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow));
  67:         if(rejection==null && priceEvents.laterApplied(symbol,observedAt))rejection="OUT_OF_ORDER";
  68:         if(rejection!=null){priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"NON_CANONICAL",rejection);return;}
  69:         onPrice(symbol,price);
  70:         // No-position and all successful no-op exits reach this statement; exceptions do not.
  71:         priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"APPLIED","PROTECTION_CALLBACK_COMPLETED");
  72:     }

  74:     @Transactional
  75:     public void onPrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal price) {
  76:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) return;
  77:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  78: 
  79:         // FIX-124: classify ONLY the first lock failure. The caller may retry after
  80:         // rollback in a fresh transaction; failures after evaluation are not retried.
  81:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
  82:         WalletManagedPosition managed;
  83:         try {
  84:             managed = managedPositionRepository
  85:                     .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  86:                     .orElse(null);
  87:         } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
  88:             if (com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure)) {
  89:                 throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
  90:             }
  91:             throw failure;
  92:         }
  93:         if (managed == null || managed.getQuantity() == null || managed.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) return;
  94: 
  95:         TradeSignal one = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "1m").orElse(null);
  96:         TradeSignal five = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "5m").orElse(null);
  97:         TradeSignal hour = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "1h").orElse(null);
  98: 
  99:         // Reaching TP is a management checkpoint, not an unconditional full exit.
 100:         if (managed.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {
 101:             // Pass the complete immutable BUY thesis. PositionContinuationPolicy and
 102:             // PositionManagementService now evaluate the same deterioration pressure (FIX-011).
 103:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(one, five, hour,
 104:                     managed.getEntryTrendScore(), managed.getEntryStructureScore(),
 105:                     managed.getEntryMomentumScore(), managed.getEntryVolumeScore(),
 106:                     managed.getEntryConfidence(), managed.getEntryTotalScore());
 107:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 108:                 BigDecimal distance = managed.getTakeProfitUsdt().subtract(managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
 109:                 BigDecimal oldTarget = managed.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 110:                 BigDecimal newTarget = oldTarget.add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 111:                 Instant changedAt = Instant.now();
 112: 
 113:                 // FIX-118 behavioral phase: if Profit Lock was already earned under the old TP
 114:                 // but the wider approved target moves current progress back below activation,
 115:                 // persist TP_EXTENSION_REBASE before saving the new geometry. The historical
 116:                 // lock remains stored but is non-executable until new-geometry activation is
 117:                 // earned again.
 118:                 DynamicProfitLockService.ExtensionTransition fix118Transition =
 119:                         dynamicProfitLockService.onTakeProfitExtended(managed, newTarget, price, changedAt);
 120:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][TP_EXTENSION] positionId={}, symbol={}, marketPrice={}, entry={}, oldTp={}, newTp={}, " +
 121:                                 "highest={}, profitLockActive={}, profitLockPrice={}, previousState={}, state={}, " +
 122:                                 "newGeometryProgressPct={}, activationPct={}",
 123:                         managed.getId(), symbol, price, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), oldTarget, newTarget,
 124:                         managed.getHighestPriceUsdt(), managed.isProfitLockActive(), managed.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(),
 125:                         fix118Transition.previousState(), fix118Transition.state(), fix118Transition.progressPercent(),
 126:                         fix118Transition.activationPercent());
 127: 
 128:                 managed.setTakeProfitUsdt(newTarget);
 129:                 // FIX-11T: TP extension changes the planned entry->TP distance. Any old Near-TP
 130:                 // arm/rejection was relative to the superseded target and must be restarted.
 131:                 resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(managed);
 132:                 managed.setUpdatedAt(changedAt);
 133:                 managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 134: 
 135:                 // FIX-067: Production has two persisted views of the same open lifecycle:
 136:                 // wallet_managed_position drives live protection, while paper_position is also
 137:                 // evaluated when a fresh TradeSignal arrives.  Before this fix only the managed
 138:                 // position received a TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED update.  The next 1m signal could
 139:                 // therefore see the stale ORIGINAL paper TP and close the wallet early.
 140:                 // PEPE wallet #833 / managed position #552 proved the race: managed TP was
 141:                 // extended 0.000004146897 -> 0.000004165346, but the signal path sold at
 142:                 // 0.000004150 because paper_position still held 0.000004146897.
 143:                 // Keep both Production state holders synchronized inside this same transaction.
 144:                 PaperPosition synchronizedPaper = paperPositionRepository
 145:                         .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN)
 146:                         .orElse(null);
 147:                 if (synchronizedPaper != null) {
 148:                     synchronizedPaper.setTakeProfit(newTarget);
 149:                     paperPositionRepository.save(synchronizedPaper);
 150:                 }
 151: 
 152:                 // FIX-053: Persist every TP revision so Dashboard can show the actual
 153:                 // Production management path instead of only the latest target value.
 154:                 positionManagementEventRepository.save(PositionManagementEvent.builder()
 155:                         .walletPositionId(managed.getId())
 156:                         .symbol(symbol)
 157:                         .eventType("TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED")
 158:                         .oldValueUsdt(oldTarget)
 159:                         .newValueUsdt(newTarget)
 160:                         .marketPriceUsdt(price)
 161:                         .reason(continuation.explanation())
 162:                         .occurredAt(changedAt)
 163:                         .build());
 164:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT extended: symbol={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, reason={}", symbol, oldTarget, newTarget, continuation.explanation());
 165:             } else if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 166:                     symbol, price, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation())) {
 167:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), one);
 168:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT executed: symbol={}, price={}, target={}", symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 169:             }
 170:             return;
 171:         }
 172: 
 173:         // Stop loss is absolute and is never softened by continuation logic.
 174:         if (managed.getStopLossUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
 175:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 176:                     symbol, price, "STOP_LOSS",
 177:                     "Live price " + price + " reached stop loss " + managed.getStopLossUsdt())) {
 178:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.STOPPED, "STOP_LOSS",
 179:                         "Live market price reached the configured stop loss.", one);
 180:                 log.info("Live STOP_LOSS executed: symbol={}, price={}, stop={}", symbol, price, managed.getStopLossUsdt());
 181:             }
 182:             return;
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation lock = dynamicProfitLockService.evaluatePrice(symbol, price);
 186: 
 187:         // FIX-118: during TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the superseded lock is intentionally
 188:         // non-executable, but earned profit is not left completely unprotected. Stop Loss
 189:         // has already had absolute priority above; this standalone floor is the next safety
 190:         // authority and does not depend on the old lock being triggered.
 191:         if (lock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 192:             String reason = lock.explanation();
 193:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}, target={}",
 194:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.hardProfitFloor(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 195:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 196:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 197:             }
 198:             return;
 199:         }
 200: 
 201:         if (lock.active() || lock.triggered() || lock.rebasing()) {
 202:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_PATH] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, target={}, highest={}, active={}, " +
 203:                             "progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 204:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(), lock.highestPrice(), lock.active(),
 205:                     lock.progressPercent(), lock.activationPercent(), lock.lockPrice(), lock.state(), lock.triggered());
 206:         }
 207:         if (lock.triggered()) {
 208:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_TRIGGER] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, target={}",
 209:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lock.progressPercent(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 210:             BigDecimal hardProfitFloor = managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
 211:             if (price.compareTo(hardProfitFloor) < 0) {
 212:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " Hard protected-profit floor " + hardProfitFloor + " was breached.";
 213:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 214:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 215:                 }
 216:                 return;
 217:             }
 218: 
 219:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(one, five, hour);
 220:             if (lockDecision.exit()) {
 221:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " " + lockDecision.explanation();
 222:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, lockDecision.code(), reason)) {
 223:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, lockDecision.code(), reason, one);
 224:                     log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK exit: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 225:                             symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.code());
 226:                 }
 227:                 return;
 228:             }
 229:             log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK protected hold: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 230:                     symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.explanation());
 231:         }
 232: 
 233:         // Normal signal exits remain active even when Profit Lock is disabled. Higher-timeframe
 234:         // failure can therefore close a winner instead of waiting forever for an exact 1m SELL event.
 235:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(one, five, hour);
 236:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 237:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation())) {
 238:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation(), one);
 239:                 log.info("Live signal-driven exit: symbol={}, price={}, reason={}", symbol, price, normalExit.code());
 240:             }
 241:             // FIX-11T: any higher-priority position action owns this evaluation cycle.
 242:             // Near-TP must never race another SELL or quantity-changing decision on the same tick.
 243:             return;
 244:         }
 245: 
 246:         // FIX-11T: Near-TP Failure Protection is deliberately LAST. TP/SL/profit-lock and
 247:         // existing signal exits above retain their exact authority and always win the cycle.
 248:         evaluateNearTpProtection(managed, price, one, five);
 249:     }

 251:     private void evaluateNearTpProtection(WalletManagedPosition managed, BigDecimal price,
 252:                                           TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 253:         // FIX-11T evidence is explicitly time-safe. Normal Production logic above keeps its
 254:         // existing signal lookup unchanged; Near-TP alone falls back to an at-or-before query
 255:         // if the repository's newest row is future-dated relative to this live evaluation.
 256:         Instant evaluatedAt = Instant.now();
 257:         oneMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "1m", oneMinute, evaluatedAt);
 258:         fiveMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "5m", fiveMinute, evaluatedAt);
 259: 
 260:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 261:                 managed.getNearTpState() == null ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState(),
 262:                 managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), managed.getNearTpBearishStreak(),
 263:                 managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed());
 264: 
 265:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 266:                 state, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(),
 267:                 price, evaluatedAt, oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 268: 
 269:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State next = evaluation.state();
 270:         boolean stateChanged = managed.getNearTpState() != next.nearTpState()
===== lines 270- =====
 270:         boolean stateChanged = managed.getNearTpState() != next.nearTpState()
 271:                 || !sameDecimal(managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), next.bestPrice())
 272:                 || managed.getNearTpBearishStreak() != next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute()
 273:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 274:         if (stateChanged) {
 275:             managed.setNearTpState(next.nearTpState());
 276:             managed.setNearTpBestPrice(next.bestPrice());
 277:             managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute());
 278:             managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 279:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 280:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 281:         }
 282: 
 283:         if (evaluation.transition() || stateChanged) {
 284:             log.info("FIX-11T Near-TP evaluation: positionId={}, symbol={}, state={}, code={}, price={}, bestPrice={}, " +
 285:                             "tpProgressPct={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, oneMinuteId={}, oneMinuteOriginal={}, " +
 286:                             "fiveMinuteId={}, fiveMinuteOriginal={}, detail={}",
 287:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), next.nearTpState(), evaluation.code(), price, next.bestPrice(),
 288:                     evaluation.tpProgressPercent(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute(),
 289:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getId(),
 290:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 291:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getId(),
 292:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 293:                     evaluation.explanation());
 294:         } else if (log.isDebugEnabled() && evaluation.code().startsWith("HOLD_")) {
 295:             log.debug("FIX-11T Near-TP hold: positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, price={}, detail={}",
 296:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 297:         }
 298: 
 299:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return;
 300: 
 301:         WalletAutoExecutionService.PartialHarvestResult harvest = walletAutoExecutionService
 302:                 .executeNearTpPartialHarvest(managed.getSymbol(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 303:         if (!harvest.executed()) {
 304:             log.warn("FIX-11T Near-TP harvest not executed: positionId={}, symbol={}, status={}, price={}",
 305:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), harvest.status(), price);
 306:             // If execution did not happen, return to rejection monitoring rather than leaving
 307:             // the state stranded in FAILURE_CONFIRMED. Idempotency still lives in wallet_trade.
 308:             managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED);
 309:             if ("BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM".equals(harvest.status())
 310:                     || "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE".equals(harvest.status())) {
 311:                 // The skipped order consumed the current confirmation pair. Requiring fresh
 312:                 // bearish evidence prevents a retry on every subsequent market-price tick.
 313:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 314:             }
 315:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 316:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 317:             return;
 318:         }
 319: 
 320:         // Keep paper_position quantity/P&L synchronized with the managed wallet lifecycle.
 321:         // Without this, a later terminal signal close would calculate P&L on pre-harvest quantity.
 322:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(managed.getSymbol(), PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 323:         if (paper != null) {
 324:             paper.setQuantity(harvest.remainingQuantity());
 325:             BigDecimal priorRealized = paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl();
 326:             paper.setRealizedPnl(priorRealized.add(harvest.realizedPnlUsdt(), MC));
 327:             paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 328:         }
 329: 
 330:         log.info("FIX-11T NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, " +
 331:                         "price={}, realizedPnl={}, realizedPnlPct={}",
 332:                 managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), harvest.soldQuantity(), harvest.remainingQuantity(),
 333:                 price, harvest.realizedPnlUsdt(), harvest.realizedPnlPercent());
 334:     }

 336:     private TradeSignal latestEligibleSignal(String symbol, String interval, TradeSignal newest, Instant evaluatedAt) {
 337:         if (newest != null && newest.getGeneratedAt() != null && !newest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(evaluatedAt)) {
 338:             return newest;
 339:         }
 340:         return tradeSignalRepository
 341:                 .findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, evaluatedAt)
 342:                 .orElse(null);
 343:     }
 344: 
 345:     /**
 346:      * FIX-117: TP extension must not erase Near-TP protection that was already earned.
 347:      *
 348:      * Only ARMED and REJECTION_DETECTED change from the pre-FIX-117 behavior. INACTIVE
 349:      * still resets against the new geometry. FAILURE_CONFIRMED also keeps the existing
 350:      * reset behavior because that persisted/transient lifecycle has a real interruption
 351:      * window between state save and harvest resolution and is intentionally out of scope.
 352:      * PARTIAL_HARVESTED remains terminal through the existing harvest-used guard.
 353:      *
 354:      * plannedDistance is recalculated from the current TP by NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy
 355:      * on every evaluation, so the historical best price remains a valid market observation
 356:      * and does not need synthetic rebasing after an extension.
 357:      */
 358:     private void resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(WalletManagedPosition managed) {
 359:         if (managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed()) return;
 360: 
 361:         NearTpState currentState = managed.getNearTpState() == null
 362:                 ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState();
 363:         switch (currentState) {
 364:             case INACTIVE, NEAR_TP_FAILURE_CONFIRMED -> {
 365:                 // Existing behavior: no earned protection is carried into the new geometry.
 366:                 managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.INACTIVE);
 367:                 managed.setNearTpBestPrice(null);
 368:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 369:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
 370:             }
 371:             case NEAR_TP_ARMED -> {
 372:                 // FIX-117: preserve earned protection and the real historical best price.
 373:             }
 374:             case NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED -> {
 375:                 // Preserve earned protection/best price, but discard bearish persistence
 376:                 // accumulated under the old, tighter geometry. The next evaluation will
 377:                 // recalculate giveback against the new TP and either recover or remain rejected.
 378:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 379:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
 380:             }
 381:             case NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED -> {
 382:                 // Existing terminal behavior; normally returned above by nearTpHarvestUsed.
 383:             }
 384:         }
 385:     }
 386: 
 387:     private boolean sameDecimal(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
 388:         return a == null ? b == null : b != null && a.compareTo(b) == 0;
 389:     }
 390: 
 391:     private void closePaper(String symbol, BigDecimal exitPrice, PositionStatus status,
 392:                             String closeReason, String explanation, TradeSignal sourceSignal) {
 393:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 394:         if (paper == null) return;
 395:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(paper.getEntryPrice(), MC).multiply(paper.getQuantity(), MC);
 396:         // FIX-11T: paper_position may already contain realized P&L from a Near-TP partial
 397:         // harvest. Preserve it when the remaining quantity is later closed.
 398:         BigDecimal pnl = (paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl())
 399:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 400:         paper.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 401:         paper.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 402:         paper.setStatus(status);
 403:         paper.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 404:         paper.setExitReason(explanation);
 405:         paper.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 406:         PaperPosition saved = paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 407: 
 408:         // FIX-028: live-price exits (TP/SL/Profit Lock/normal exit) also write the
 409:         // immutable audit record. sourceSignal is context only unless the closeTrigger
 410:         // itself is a genuine SELL decision. No protection/exit policy is changed here.
 411:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, sourceSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 412:     }

  52:     /** Position management; optional injection preserves constructor-based policy tests. */

  55: 
  56:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
  57:     public PaperPosition openFromLatestSignal(String symbol) {
  58:         String normalized = normalizeSymbol(symbol);
  59:         TradeSignal signal = signalRepository
  60:                 .findTopBySymbolOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(normalized)
  61:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("No signal found for " + normalized));
  62: 
  63:         if (processingCoordinator != null) {
  64:             return processingCoordinator.process(signal.getId(), false, fresh -> {
  65:                 Optional<PaperPosition> result = processSignalBody(fresh);
  66:                 if (result.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("The latest signal did not open a new position.");
  67:                 return result;
  68:             }).orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("Signal was not claimed for processing."));
  69:         }
  70:         return processSignalBody(signal)
  71:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException(
  72:                         "The latest signal did not open a new position. It may be WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL, " +
  73:                                 "or a position for this symbol is already open."
  74:                 ));
  75:     }
  76: 
  77:     /**
  78:      * Applies the complete paper-trade lifecycle for one newly-created signal.
  79:      * BUY opens one position, WATCH/NEUTRAL holds it, and SELL closes it.
  80:      * Stop-loss and take-profit are checked before the signal decision.
  81:      */
  82:     // FIX-127: mandatory in Spring. Null only supports existing manually constructed
  83:     // business-policy unit tests; Production cannot start without the coordinator.
  84:     @Autowired
  85:     private com.crypto.execution.processing.SignalProcessingCoordinator processingCoordinator;
  86: 
  87:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
  88:     public Optional<PaperPosition> processSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
  89:         if (processingCoordinator == null) return processSignalBody(signal);
  90:         if (signal == null || signal.getId() == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Persisted signal required");
  91:         return processingCoordinator.process(signal.getId(), false, this::processSignalBody);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
  95:     public Optional<PaperPosition> recoverRegisteredSignal(long signalId) {
  96:         return processingCoordinator.process(signalId, true, this::processSignalBody);
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     /** FIX-132: explicit delivery ownership, never a natural-key inference. */
 100:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
 101:     public Optional<PaperPosition> processSharedSignal(TradeSignal signal, long sourceEventId) {
 102:         return processingCoordinator.process(signal.getId(),false,this::processSignalBody,sourceEventId);
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     /** FIX-127: existing decision order; invoked inside the coordinator's transaction. */
 106:     private Optional<PaperPosition> processSignalBody(TradeSignal signal) {
 107:         if (signal == null) {
 108:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Trade signal is required");
 109:         }
 110: 
 111:         String symbol = normalizeSymbol(signal.getSymbol());
 112: 
 113:         // FIX-127: position management persists advice and can request POSITION_STOP_LOSS.
 114:         // Keep its existing decision order, but acquire the initial managed lock before entering here.
 115:         if (positionManagementService != null) {
 116:             try {
 117:                 positionManagementService.analyze(signal);
 118:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 119:                 log.warn("Position advisory failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage());
 120:             }
 121:         }
 122:         Optional<PaperPosition> openPosition = positionRepository
 123:                 .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN);
 124: 
 125:         if (openPosition.isPresent()) {
 126:             PaperPosition position = openPosition.get();
 127:             BigDecimal price = signal.getLatestPrice();
 128: 
 129:             // Never treat a delayed timeframe candle close as the current mark price.
 130:             // Context from that signal is still processed below, but TP/SL/profit-lock
 131:             // mechanics require a post-entry market observation. Live production protection
 132:             // is independently driven by LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice(...).
 133:             boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, position.getOpenedAt());
 134:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock = authoritativePrice
 135:                     ? dynamicProfitLockService.evaluate(signal)
 136:                     : DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation.inactive(
 137:                             "Signal price predates the open position and is context-only for mechanical protection.");
 138: 
 139:             // FIX-118 Production trace for the independent signal-price Profit Lock authority.
 140:             // The explicit ProfitLockState now also shows whether the historical lock is ACTIVE
 141:             // or temporarily non-executable while TP extension is rebasing.
 142:             if (authoritativePrice && (profitLock.active() || profitLock.triggered() || profitLock.rebasing())) {
 143:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_PATH] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, price={}, " +
 144:                                 "active={}, progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 145:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, signal.getInterval(), price,
 146:                         profitLock.active(), profitLock.progressPercent(), profitLock.activationPercent(),
 147:                         profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.state(), profitLock.triggered());
 148:             }
 149: 
 150:             // FIX-067: The wallet-managed position is the authoritative Production source for
 151:             // a dynamically extended take-profit.  paper_position historically retained the
 152:             // entry-time TP, so a fresh 1m signal could close against that stale threshold even
 153:             // after LivePositionProtectionService had persisted TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED.
 154:             // Prefer the OPEN managed TP and repair the paper snapshot when they differ. This is
 155:             // deliberately NOT a TP-policy change: it only makes both existing exit paths compare
 156:             // against the same already-approved target.
 157:             BigDecimal authoritativeTakeProfit = position.getTakeProfit();
 158:             WalletManagedPosition managedPosition = walletManagedPositionRepository
 159:                     .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 160:                     .orElse(null);
 161:             if (managedPosition != null && managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null) {
 162:                 authoritativeTakeProfit = managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 163:                 if (position.getTakeProfit() == null
 164:                         || position.getTakeProfit().compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) != 0) {
 165:                     position.setTakeProfit(authoritativeTakeProfit);
 166:                     positionRepository.save(position);
 167:                 }
 168:             }
 169: 
 170:             if (authoritativePrice && authoritativeTakeProfit != null
 171:                     && price.compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) >= 0) {
 172:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 173:                         "TAKE_PROFIT", "Price reached the authoritative configured take-profit target."));
 174:             }
 175: 
 176:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.triggered()) {
 177:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}",
 178:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.progressPercent());
 179:                 return Optional.of(closeFromProfitLock(position, signal, profitLock));
 180:             }
 181: 
 182:             if (authoritativePrice && price.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0) {
 183:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.STOPPED,
 184:                         "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the configured stop loss."));
 185:             }
 186: 
 187:             // FIX-118: in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the old Profit Lock cannot execute, but the
 188:             // direct protected-profit floor still applies. Stop Loss remains absolute and
 189:             // therefore keeps priority immediately above this branch.
 190:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 191:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}",
 192:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.hardProfitFloor());
 193:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 194:                         "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", profitLock.explanation()));
 195:             }
 196: 
 197:             if (signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.SELL
 198:                     || signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
 199:                 if (profitLock.active()) {
 200:                     log.info("Normal wallet SELL suppressed because Dynamic Profit Lock is active: signalId={}, symbol={}, protectedPrice={}, currentPrice={}",
 201:                             signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), profitLock.lockPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
 202:                     return Optional.of(position);
 203:                 }
 204:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validation =
 205:                         executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
 206:                 if (validation.allowed()) {
 207:                     return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 208:                             signal.getDecision().name(), signal.getExplanation()));
 209:                 }
 210:                 log.info("Normal wallet SELL rejected: signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, reason={}, detail={}",
 211:                         signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(),
 212:                         validation.code(), validation.explanation());
 213:             }
 214: 
 215:             // Progressive Position Building may add to an existing position only after
 216:             // TP / Profit Lock / SL / validated SELL logic had first priority.
 217:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && !profitLock.active()) {
 218:                 WalletManagedPosition managed = walletManagedPositionRepository
 219:                         .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 220:                         .orElse(null);
 420:         return processSignal(signal);
 421:     }
 422: 
 423:     @Transactional
 424:     public PaperPosition close(Long positionId, BigDecimal exitPrice) {
 425:         PaperPosition position = positionRepository.findById(positionId)
 426:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Position not found"));
 427: 
 428:         if (position.getStatus() != PositionStatus.OPEN) {
 429:             throw new IllegalStateException("Position is already closed");
 430:         }
 431: 
 432:         return completeClose(position, exitPrice,
 433:                 exitPrice.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0 ? PositionStatus.STOPPED : PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 434:                 "MANUAL_CLOSE", "Position was manually closed.", null);
 435:     }
 436: 
 437:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 438:     public List<PaperPosition> list() {
 439:         return positionRepository.findTop100ByOrderByOpenedAtDesc();
 440:     }
 441: 
 442:     private PaperPosition closeFromSignal(
 443:             PaperPosition position,
 444:             TradeSignal signal,
 445:             PositionStatus status,
 446:             String closeReason,
 447:             String explanation
 448:     ) {
 449:         return completeClose(position, signal.getLatestPrice(), status, closeReason, explanation, signal);
 450:     }
 451: 
 452:     private PaperPosition closeFromProfitLock(
 453:             PaperPosition position,
 454:             TradeSignal signal,
 455:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock
 456:     ) {
 457:         BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
 458:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 459:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 461:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 462:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 463: 
 464:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 465:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 466:         position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
 467:         position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
 468:         position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
 469:         position.setExitSignal(signal);
 470:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 471:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 472: 
 473:         // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
 474:         // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
 475:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
 476: 
 477:         try {
 478:             walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
 479:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 480:             log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 481:         }
 482:         // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
 483:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
 484:         return saved;
 485:     }
 486: 
 487:     private PaperPosition completeClose(
 488:             PaperPosition position,
 489:             BigDecimal exitPrice,
 490:             PositionStatus status,
 491:             String closeReason,
 492:             String explanation,
 493:             TradeSignal exitSignal
 494:     ) {
 495:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 496:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 497:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 498:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 499:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 500: 
 501:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 502:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 503:         position.setStatus(status);
 504:         position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 505:         position.setExitReason(explanation);
 506:         position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
 507:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 508:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 509: 
 510:         // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
 511:         // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
 512:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);
 513: 
 514:         if (exitSignal != null) {
 515:             try {
 516:                 // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
 517:                 // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
 518:                 // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
 519:                 // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
 520:                 if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
 521:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
 522:                 } else {
 523:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 524:                 }
 525:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 526:                 log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 527:             }
 528:         }
 529: 
 530:         // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
 531:         // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
 532:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 533:         return saved;
 534:     }

 537:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 538:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 539:         }
 540:         return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();

  53:     // FIX-124: no suspended caller transaction may retain market-data locks.
  54:     @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.NEVER)
  55:     public boolean processKline(JsonNode root) {
  56:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: Trader kline ingestion is disabled"); }
  57:         if(drain!=null && !drain.beginInput()){log.info("[FIX-153][KLINE_REJECTED_DRAINING]");return false;}
  58:         try {
  59: 
  60:         JsonNode data = root.has("data")
  61:                 ? root.path("data")
  62:                 : root;
  63: 
  64:         JsonNode kline = data.path("k");
  65: 
  66:         if (kline.isMissingNode() || kline.isNull()) {
  67:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
  68:                     "Binance message does not contain kline data"
  69:             );
  70:         }
  71: 
  72:         String symbol = kline.path("s")
  73:                 .asText()
  74:                 .trim()

  76: 
  77:         String intervalCode = kline.path("i")
  78:                 .asText()
  79:                 .trim();
  80: 
  81:         Instant openTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
  82:                 kline.path("t").asLong()
  83:         );
  84: 
  85:         Instant closeTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
  86:                 kline.path("T").asLong()
  87:         );
  88: 
  89:         boolean closed = kline.path("x")
  90:                 .asBoolean(false);
  91:         BigDecimal livePrice = MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(decimal(kline, "c"));
  92: 
  93:         // FIX-153: an absent Binance event timestamp is not equivalent to a fresh receipt.
  94:         Instant observedAt = data.path("E").asLong(0L)>0 ? Instant.ofEpochMilli(data.path("E").asLong()) : null;
  95:         boolean canonical="1m".equals(intervalCode);
  96:         String token=java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
  97:         java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<CandleRepository.Transition> transition=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
  98:         java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<Long> priceId=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
  99:         long started=System.nanoTime();
 100:         try {
 101:             transactions.process(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,livePrice,
 102:                 ()->{
 103:                     transition.set(candleRepository.upsertRuntime(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,
 104:                         decimal(kline,"o"),decimal(kline,"h"),decimal(kline,"l"),livePrice,

 106:                     if(canonical)priceId.set(marketPriceEventService.recordPending(symbol,livePrice,observedAt));
 107:                 },

 114:             if(closed && completedTransition!=null && !completedTransition.transitionedToClosed()) {
 115:                 com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DUPLICATE_CLOSE);

 118:                 // This audit is intentionally after the ingestion transaction and
 119:                 // fail-safe: an audit problem must never make a Binance receipt fail.

 196:                     if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
 197:                     persistSell(runId, symbol, signal, open, exit, pnl, pnlPct);
 198:                     closePosition(runId, open.positionId(), signal, exit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
 199:                     // FIX-020 replay parity: a terminal replay exit consumes the same
 200:                     // opportunity evidence boundary as production before any new BUY can form.

 202:                     executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, signal, exit.reason());

 204:                     // Production PaperTradingService returns immediately after a signal-driven
 205:                     // terminal close; it cannot reopen from that same signal invocation.
 206:                     continue;
 207:                 } else {

 450:                 || current.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()

 547:             }

 562:     private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
 563:         BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
 564:         if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
 565: 
 566:         // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
 567:         // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
 568:         // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
 569:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
 570:         if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 571:             // Exact production TP-continuation call, including immutable entry structure.
 572:             // FIX-011 regression relies on Replay and live using this same shared policy.
 573:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 574:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 575:                     p.entryTrend(), p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(),
 576:                     p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());
 577:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 578:                 BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
 579:                 BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 580:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
 581:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
 582:                 return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
 583:             }
 584:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
 585:                     runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 586:                     p.entryTime(), p.entryPrice(), p.quantity(), p.cost(), p.stopLoss(), p.takeProfit(), p.highest(),
 587:                     p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.entryScore(), p.entryConfidence(), p.entryTrend(),
 588:                     p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(), oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 589:                     continuation.explanation());
 590:             persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), p, continuation.explanation());
 591:             return new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation());
 592:         }
 593:         // FIX-052 fallback ordering also mirrors Production: STOP_LOSS is absolute and
 594:         // is evaluated before profit-lock. Exact V64 runs normally take this path from
 595:         // persisted live-price events rather than from signal-carried prices.
 596:         if (authoritativePrice && p.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(p.stopLoss()) <= 0)
 597:             return new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the stored stop loss.");
 598: 
 599:         ShadowPosition updated = authoritativePrice ? profitLockState(p, price) : p;
 600:         if (authoritativePrice && (updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE
 601:                 || updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 602:                 || p.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()
 603:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice())
 604:                 || p.profitLockState() != updated.profitLockState())) {
 605:             BigDecimal fix118Progress = updated.takeProfit() == null || updated.takeProfit().compareTo(updated.entryPrice()) <= 0
 606:                     ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 607:                     : updated.highest().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC)
 608:                             .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 609:                             .divide(updated.takeProfit().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 610:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_PATH] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, " +
 611:                             "entry={}, target={}, highest={}, previousActive={}, active={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousLock={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, triggered={}",
 612:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 613:                     updated.entryPrice(), updated.takeProfit(), updated.highest(), p.profitLockActive(), updated.profitLockActive(),
 614:                     p.profitLockState(), updated.profitLockState(), p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice(), fix118Progress,
 615:                     updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 616:                             && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0);
 617:         }
 618:         BigDecimal minimumProfitableExit = p.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
 619:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 620:                 && price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 621:             String floorReason = "TP-extension rebase safety floor was breached at " + minimumProfitableExit + ". "
 622:                     + profitLockConfigText();
 623:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, floor={}",
 624:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price, minimumProfitableExit);
 625:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 626:             return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 627:         }
 628:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 629:                 && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
 630:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, lock={}, target={}",
 631:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 632:                     updated.profitLockPrice(), updated.takeProfit());
 633:             if (price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 634:                 String floorReason = "Profit-lock hard floor was breached; protected profit can no longer be preserved. "
 635:                         + profitLockConfigText();
 636:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 637:                 return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 638:             }
 639:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(
 640:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 641:             if (!lockDecision.exit()) {
 642:                 String holdReason = lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 643:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, holdReason);
 644:                 return ExitDecision.hold();
 645:             }
 646:             String lockReason = "Price retraced to the protected profit level after a profitable advance. "
 647:                     + lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 648:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, lockReason);
 649:             return new ExitDecision(true, lockDecision.code(), lockReason);
 650:         }
 651:         // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the
 652:         // 1m signal TradeExecutionValidationService. Replay executes both shared production
 653:         // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.
 654:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(
 655:                 oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 656:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 657:             return new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation());
 658:         }
 659:         if ("1m".equals(s.getInterval()) && bearish(s.getDecision())) {
 660:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(s);
 661:             if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
 662:                 return new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
 663:             }
 664:         }
 665:         return ExitDecision.hold();
 666:     }

 164:                 logSaved(sourceEventId,event,historical,"HISTORY");
 165:                 return terminal(event,"HISTORICAL_ONLY","HISTORICAL_ANALYSIS", "HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 166:             }
 167:             stage = "LIVE_SIGNAL_AND_REGISTRATION";
 168:             traceStage(sourceEventId,event,stage,started);
 169:             TradeSignal signal = sourceEventId == null
 170:                 ? analysisService.analyzeForProcessing(indicator, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.WORKER)
 171:                 : analysisService.analyzeForProcessing(indicator, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.WORKER,sourceEventId);
 172:             logSaved(sourceEventId,event,signal,"LIVE");
 173:             stage = "SIGNAL_PROCESSING";
 174:             traceStage(sourceEventId,event,stage,started);
 175:             Optional<PaperPosition> position = sourceEventId == null ? paperTradingService.processSignal(signal)
 176:                     : paperTradingService.processSharedSignal(signal,sourceEventId);
 177: 
 178:             log.info(
 179:                     "Automatic candle flow completed: symbol={}, interval={}, openTime={}, score={}, decision={}, paperPositionOpened={}",
 180:                     indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime(),
 181:                     signal.getTotalScore(), signal.getDecision(), position.isPresent());
 182:             // FIX-153: Optional.empty is not a completed/expired execution acknowledgment.
 183:             return sourceEventId==null?localProcessingOutcome(signal.getId()):processingOutcome(sourceEventId);
 184:         } catch (com.crypto.execution.processing.SymbolLockRetryScheduled scheduled) {
 185:             return sourceEventId==null?"REVIEW_REQUIRED":processingOutcome(sourceEventId);
 186:         } catch (Exception exception) {
 187:             // FIX-043: never let one analysis failure kill the dispatcher lane. The next candle must
 188:             // continue. FIX-132 LIVE persists this failure for reconciliation; legacy
 189:             // recovery must not independently execute an uncertain shared outcome.
 190:             log.error("[FIX-137][WORKER_STAGE_FAILED] event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, open={}, stage={}, elapsedMs={}",
 191:                     sourceEventId,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),stage,
 192:                     (System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000,exception);
 193:             log.error("Automatic candle flow failed for {} {} at {}",
 194:                     event.symbol(), event.intervalCode(), event.openTime(), exception);
 195:             if(sourceEventId != null) {
 196:                 try {
 197:                     String detail=exception.getClass().getName()+": "+exception.getMessage();
 198:                     sharedWorkEvidence.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET last_error=? WHERE source_event_id=? AND analysis_status IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED')",
 199:                             detail.substring(0,Math.min(1000,detail.length())),sourceEventId);
 200:                 } catch(Exception recordingFailure) {
 201:                     log.error("[FIX-136][ANALYSIS_ERROR_RECORD_FAILED] event={}",sourceEventId,recordingFailure);
 202:                 }
 203:             }
 204:             return "REVIEW_REQUIRED";
 205:         }
 206:     }
 207:     /** FIX-138: emitted only after the transactional service proxy returned.
 208:      * Signal persistence is distinct from wallet processing and delivery completion. */
 209:     private static void logSaved(Long eventId,CandleClosedEvent event,TradeSignal signal,String mode) {
 210:         if(signal==null || signal.getId()==null) {
 211:             log.warn("[FIX-138][SIGNAL_SAVE_UNCONFIRMED] mode={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}; service returned no persisted signal identity",
 212:                 mode,eventId,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode());
 213:             return;
 214:         }
 215:         java.time.Instant returned=java.time.Instant.now();
 216:         Long liveDelayMs="LIVE".equals(mode)&&event.closeTime()!=null&&signal.getGeneratedAt()!=null
 217:             ?java.time.Duration.between(event.closeTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt()).toMillis():null;
 218:         Long insertionDelayMs="LIVE".equals(mode)&&event.closeTime()!=null&&signal.getCreatedAt()!=null
 219:             ?java.time.Duration.between(event.closeTime(),signal.getCreatedAt()).toMillis():null;
 220:         log.info("[FIX-153][SIGNAL_SAVED] mode={}, event={}, signal={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenUtc={}, candleCloseUtc={}, generatedUtc={}, rowCreatedUtc={}, serviceReturnedUtc={}, serviceReturnedKsa={}, liveCloseToGenerationMs={}, liveCloseToInsertionMs={}; rowCreatedUtc is row creation time, not database commit time; historical generatedUtc is an as-of clock, signal save does not imply a wallet trade",
 221:             mode,eventId,signal.getId(),event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),event.closeTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt(),signal.getCreatedAt(),returned,returned.atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)),liveDelayMs,insertionDelayMs);
 222:     }

 375: 
 376:     /**
 377:      * Applies a Dynamic Profit Lock exit generated by the Position Manager.
 378:      * The source signal is used only for traceability; it does not need to be a SELL signal.
 379:      */
 380:     @Transactional
 381:     public boolean executeProfitLock(TradeSignal sourceSignal, BigDecimal executionPrice, BigDecimal protectedPrice) {
 382:         if (sourceSignal == null || sourceSignal.getId() == null || executionPrice == null || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
 383:             return false;
 384:         }
 385: 
 386:         String pair = normalizePair(sourceSignal.getSymbol());
 387:         if(walletCoordination!=null) { walletCoordination.symbol(pair); }
 388:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
 389:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN")
 390:                 .orElse(null);
 391:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
 392:         // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
 393:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
 394: 
 395:         if (position == null || position.getId() == null || position.getQuantity() == null
 396:                 || position.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) {
 397:             return false;
 398:         }
 399: 
 400:         String key = "PROFIT_LOCK:" + position.getId();
 401:         if (executionExists(key)) return true;
 402: 
 403:         String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
 404:         if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
 405:         WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
 406:         BigDecimal quantity = position.getQuantity().min(coin.getQuantity());
 407:         if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return false;
 408: 
 409:         BigDecimal price = positive(executionPrice);
 410:         BigDecimal gross = quantity.multiply(price);
 411:         BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity);
 412:         BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis);
 413:         BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized
 414:                 .divide(costBasis, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 415:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 416: 
 417:         coin.setQuantity(coin.getQuantity().subtract(quantity));
 418:         if (coin.getQuantity().signum() == 0) coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(null);
 419:         assetRepository.save(coin);
 420:         // FIX-037: credit SELL proceeds atomically so a concurrent BUY cannot overwrite them.
 421:         if (assetRepository.creditQuantity("USDT", gross) != 1) {
 422:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to credit USDT wallet balance");
 423:         }
 424:         // FIX-037: keep a final post-credit balance snapshot for lambda-based daily-stat updates.
 425:         // Using a separate final variable also avoids capturing a reassigned local variable.
 426:         final BigDecimal endingUsdt = getOrCreate("USDT").getQuantity();
 427: 
 428:         position.setQuantity(ZERO);
 429:         position.setTotalCostUsdt(ZERO);
 430:         position.setAverageEntryPriceUsdt(ZERO);
 431:         position.setStatus("CLOSED");
 432:         position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 433:         managedPositionRepository.save(position);
 434: 
 435:         tradeRepository.save(WalletTrade.builder()
 436:                 .signal(sourceSignal)
 437:                 .executionKey(key)
 438:                 .symbol(pair)
 439:                 .side("SELL")
 440:                 .quantity(quantity)
 441:                 .priceUsdt(price)
 442:                 .grossAmountUsdt(gross)
 443:                 .feeUsdt(ZERO)
 444:                 .netAmountUsdt(gross)
 445:                 .costBasisUsdt(costBasis)
 446:                 .realizedPnlUsdt(realized)
 447:                 .realizedPnlPercent(realizedPercent)
 448:                 .executionType("PAPER_AUTO")
 449:                 .executionReason("POSITION_PROFIT_LOCK")
 450:                 .status("EXECUTED")
 451:                 .executedAt(Instant.now())
 452:                 .notes("Dynamic Profit Lock applied to wallet")
 453:                 .executionMessage("Dynamic Profit Lock for trade signal #" + sourceSignal.getId()
 454:                         + " sold " + quantity + " " + assetSymbol + " at " + price
 455:                         + " USDT; protected level was " + protectedPrice + " USDT")
 456:                 .build());
 457: 
 458:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC))
 459:                 .ifPresent(daily -> {
 460:                     daily.setEndingUsdt(endingUsdt);
 461:                     daily.setEndingPortfolioUsdt(walletService.currentPortfolioValue());
 462:                     daily.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 463:                     dailyStatisticsRepository.save(daily);
 464:                 });
 465:         walletService.captureSnapshot();
 466:         return true;
 467:     }
 468: 
 469: 
 470:     /**
 471:      * Executes a mechanical live-price exit (TP / SL / Profit Lock) without requiring
 472:      * a newly generated trade signal. The open wallet position is the source of truth.
 473:      */
 474:     @Transactional
 475:     public boolean executeMechanicalExit(
 476:             String symbol,
 477:             BigDecimal executionPrice,
 478:             String executionReason,
 479:             String executionMessage
 480:     ) {
 481:         String pair = normalizePair(symbol);
 482:         if(walletCoordination!=null) { walletCoordination.symbol(pair); }
 483:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
 484:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN")
 485:                 .orElse(null);
 486:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
 487:         // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
 488:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
 489: 
 490:         if (position == null || position.getId() == null || position.getQuantity() == null
 491:                 || position.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) {
 492:             return false;
 493:         }
 494: 
 495:         String reason = executionReason == null || executionReason.isBlank()
 496:                 ? "MECHANICAL_EXIT"
 497:                 : executionReason.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 498:         String key = "POSITION:" + position.getId() + ":" + reason;
 499:         if (executionExists(key)) return true;
 500: 
 501:         String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
 502:         if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
 503:         WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
 504:         BigDecimal quantity = position.getQuantity().min(coin.getQuantity());
 505:         if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return false;
 506: 
 507:         BigDecimal price = positive(executionPrice);
 508:         BigDecimal gross = quantity.multiply(price);
 509:         BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity);
 510:         BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis);
 511:         BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized
 512:                 .divide(costBasis, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 513:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 514: 
 515:         coin.setQuantity(coin.getQuantity().subtract(quantity));
 516:         if (coin.getQuantity().signum() == 0) coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(null);
 517:         assetRepository.save(coin);
 518:         // FIX-037: credit SELL proceeds atomically so a concurrent BUY cannot overwrite them.
 519:         if (assetRepository.creditQuantity("USDT", gross) != 1) {
 520:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to credit USDT wallet balance");
 521:         }
 522:         // FIX-037: keep a final post-credit balance snapshot for lambda-based daily-stat updates.
 523:         // Using a separate final variable also avoids capturing a reassigned local variable.
 524:         final BigDecimal endingUsdt = getOrCreate("USDT").getQuantity();
 525: 
 526:         position.setQuantity(ZERO);
 527:         position.setTotalCostUsdt(ZERO);
 528:         position.setAverageEntryPriceUsdt(ZERO);
 529:         position.setStatus("CLOSED");

@@ -49,0 +50,24 @@ public class LivePositionProtectionService {
+    // FIX-153: direct observations use atomic effects+outcome; shared callers retain their ledger API.
+    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired private com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService priceEvents;
+    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
+    @Transactional(isolation=org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Isolation.READ_COMMITTED)
+    public void onPrice(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observedAt,long eventId){
+        price=com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(price);
+        if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
+        // Acquire the existing managed-position lock BEFORE freshness, including waits on it.
+        try {managedPositionRepository.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol,"OPEN");}
+        catch(RuntimeException failure){
+            // FIX-153: preserve only the established pre-business position-deadlock retry permission.
+            if(com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure))
+                throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
+            throw failure;
+        }
+        Instant localNow=Instant.now();
+        String rejection=priceEvents.invalidReason(price,observedAt,exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow));
+        if(rejection==null && priceEvents.laterApplied(symbol,observedAt))rejection="OUT_OF_ORDER";
+        if(rejection!=null){priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"NON_CANONICAL",rejection);return;}
+        onPrice(symbol,price);
+        // No-position and all successful no-op exits reach this statement; exceptions do not.
+        priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"APPLIED","PROTECTION_CALLBACK_COMPLETED");
+    }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Position 35 / managed position 64 opened from signal 89469 at `2026-10-09T20:45:05.997Z` (`23:45:05.997 KSA`) and wallet trade 107 sold at `20:58:43.554Z` (`23:58:43.554 KSA`). This excludes the later 90192/65 trade.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Q1:** The system exited through the live-price `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT` path, not take-profit. The recorded TP was `0.000005433730`; persisted highest protected price was `0.000005430000`, below TP. The lock was armed at `20:58:09.534Z` / `23:58:09.534 KSA`, at `0.000005421865`; the recorded exit price was `0.000005420000`, below that lock.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The policy arithmetic is consistent: `84.2815%` TP progress activates the 70% lock and completes one 10% trail step, yielding a 50%-progress lock of `0.000005421865`. At the breach, latest context was 1m `WATCH` 89795, 5m `WATCH` 89724, and 1h `NEUTRAL` 88327. `NEUTRAL` 1h satisfies neither protected-hold branch, so the default result is `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Q2:** No continuation occurred because live continuation is evaluated only when the live price is at or above TP. That condition was not reached by the system’s accepted/protected price path, and the position closed roughly 39 minutes before the `00:38 KSA` candle on 10 October (`21:38Z`). If “12:38 KSA” means noon, the supplied evidence does not cover it.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Q3:** Protection did not forecast remaining upside after a lock breach. Its ordering is TP checkpoint → stop loss → profit-lock evaluation → lock-breach policy. Continuation is absent from the lock-breach branch. The later rise is outcome-only information.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Claude’s material correction is valid: `PaperTradingService` has a second, candle-close signal-price mechanical path. It can close at TP or profit lock without calling continuation or the higher-timeframe lock-hold policy. It did not cause this exit: the next eligible 1m signal, 89815, was generated at `20:59:02.743Z`, after trade 107 at `20:58:43.554Z`.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Had the live breach been held, signal 89815 would likely have triggered the separate signal-price profit-lock close about 19 seconds later at its stored `0.000005420000` price. This is counterfactual, not proof of an identical runtime outcome.

## EVIDENCE

## OBJECTIONS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** “TP was never reached” must mean “no protected/system-accepted price reached TP.” Protection receives Binance kline field `c`, not field `h`; it does not use candle highs for exits. [BinanceKlineService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:91) [BinanceKlineService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:104)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Exact-price Replay omits the signal-price TP/profit-lock mechanics; fallback Replay models them differently, including continuation and lock-hold policy. Therefore neither proves Production parity for a proposed continuation/hold rule. [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:183) [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:562)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** One later upswing cannot support a strategy-improvement claim. Any proposed rule needs out-of-sample testing, fees/slippage, adverse movement, and corrected Replay/Production parity.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The preserved DB bundle has no `market_price_event` rows for the trigger, and the read-only DB credential is unavailable. I therefore cannot name or independently validate the precise accepted price-event ID.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The worktree is dirty and no immutable binary hash/configuration snapshot for the pre-exit runtime is preserved. Current source is corroborative; the persisted trade 107 reason/message is the direct runtime evidence.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** There is no evidence that Binance never printed TP between received kline updates; only that this system did not act on such a price.
